docs/N105_CRYPTOGRAPHIC_VALIDATOR_IDENTITY.md:17:The phase includes the following concrete deliverables. The amun-validator-identity crate containing the ValidatorId type and derivation function, the ValidatorKeyRegistry mapping ValidatorId to public key and PeerId to ValidatorId, and the vote signing payload and Ed25519 verification functions. Modifications to the amun-consensus-network crate where ConsensusEngine gains a validator_keys field of type ValidatorKeyRegistry, along with register_validator and register_validator_identity methods, and signature verification in process_vote that is conditioned on the registry not being empty. Modifications to the amun-live-cluster crate where LiveValidator gains a real Ed25519 signing key, derives its true ValidatorId from the signing key public key, signs outgoing votes with the signing key, loads peer certificates from disk and verifies them against a test authority, and registers them in the engine. Four test certificate files generated once and stored permanently under crates/amun-live-cluster/testdata/certs. Updates to the ClusterPeer struct to carry an optional certificate_path field, and updates to the test cluster constructors to populate certificate paths automatically.
docs/N105_CRYPTOGRAPHIC_VALIDATOR_IDENTITY.md:19:## Architecture
docs/N105_CRYPTOGRAPHIC_VALIDATOR_IDENTITY.md:33:The ConsensusEngine in amun-consensus-network is modified to store a ValidatorKeyRegistry alongside existing consensus state. It provides register_validator and register_validator_identity for populating the registry. In process_vote, if the registry is non-empty, it enforces signature verification before any other checks. Votes from unknown validators or with invalid signatures are rejected. The public interface adds the validator_keys field of type ValidatorKeyRegistry, the register_validator method taking a validator_id and a public_key, and the register_validator_identity method taking a peer_id, a validator_id, and a public_key. The data flow is as follows. When a vote arrives, process_vote checks if the validator_keys registry is empty. If it is not empty, the method looks up the voter public key via validator_keys.get, computes the signing payload using vote_signing_payload, verifies the signature with verify_ed25519, returns an error if any step fails, and then proceeds with the usual height, round, and equivocation checks.
docs/N105_CRYPTOGRAPHIC_VALIDATOR_IDENTITY.md:47:## Consensus Impact
docs/N105_CRYPTOGRAPHIC_VALIDATOR_IDENTITY.md:85:The amun-validator-identity crate exists with all specified types and functions, and its own three unit tests pass. The ConsensusEngine has a validator_keys field, and process_vote enforces signature verification when the registry is non-empty. The test n105_signature_required_when_registry_populated passes. The LiveValidator generates a real signing key, derives its ValidatorId, signs votes, and loads peer certificates from files. No seed-based derivation or SigningKey from_bytes calls exist in the live cluster runtime source. No runtime fallback path exists for validator identity registration. Certificates are mandatory for all live-cluster peers. All four certificate files exist in the testdata certs directory. The full workspace builds without warnings related to N105. All existing workspace tests pass.
